# Spec revision 36: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at stage 1's
regeneration, commit `7aa9fc5` (2026-10-19; stage 1 changed no verdict against the tag `version-1`,
so this is also the change against version 1), against the record as committed now, after revision 36's one
regeneration (version 2 group G and the Holmes–Rahe event sizes, 2026-10-22). Revision 36 changed rules, so a
changed verdict here is a **new result under a new rule**, reported beside version 1's verdict and never in its
place (`M11.F`); a premise passing is never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width stage 1's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly stage 1's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from stage 1's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.

| Record | Hash at stage 1 | Hash now | Rows compared | Verdicts changed | Effects moved | Differ at all | Only at stage 1 | Only now |

| `docs/phase_d_ensemble_record.md` | `8d70109c6235` | `3549d7c45f4b` | 25 | 2 | 8 | 253 | 0 | 0 |

In the level-occupancy record, which has no verdicts, "Verdicts changed" counts the rows whose values changed.

**Not compared here:**

- `docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md`: it has no machine-readable block; `git diff 7aa9fc5 -- docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md` shows what changed.
- `docs/review/phase_c_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_c_chart_seed7.html`: one seed's run, rendered; S1.7a adds lock-in release rows by design, so `git diff 7aa9fc5` on them shows that and any change of trajectory.
- `docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7_nadia.md`: Phase B's seed-7 traces, rendered at Phase B's close (`3132c69`) and not regenerated since, so they are stage 1's.
- `docs/phase_c_completion_report.md §11`: generated from the Phase C records compared here (`tools/c16_report.py`).
- `docs/spec_coverage.md`: generated from the tests and the ensemble and mutation records compared here.
- `docs/phase_b_mutation_record.md`: the record of Phase B's close at commit `3132c69`, frozen (plan D5) and not regenerated, so it is stage 1's apart from the status line stage 1 added; `tools/mutation_gate.py` refuses to rewrite it.
- `docs/version_2_sweep_record.md, docs/version_2_status.md`: stage 2's pages, new at revision 37 and generated from the records compared here (the status page) and from the stage 2 sweep's own cache (the sweep record, `tools/version_2_sweep.py`), which has no earlier version at stage 1.
- `docs/review/group_g_readout.md`: revision 36's before-and-after measurement on 20 seeds (`tools/group_g_readout.py`), new at revision 36: its "before" section is the earlier engine's, kept as measured, and its "after" section the regenerated engine's; nothing at stage 1 to compare.
- `docs/review/stage_3_projection_trace.md, docs/review/stage_3_iposition_probe.md, docs/review/stage_3_t130_event_sizes.md, docs/review/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.md`: stage 3's measurements before any rule changed (step 2's trace and probe and step 3's displaced coping probe, `tools/stage_3_projection_trace.py`, `tools/stage_3_iposition_probe.py` and `tools/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.py`, in no record's hash) and step 1's T-130 comparison, new in stage 3; nothing at stage 1 to compare.
- `docs/review/stage_3_unit_mutation_gate.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_c2_mutants.md`: stage 3's measurements between the open batch's commits (D31): step 7's unit mutation proofs (`tools/mutation_gate.py`) and its targeted criteria and mutant runs (`tools/stage_3_targeted_run.py`), new in stage 3; nothing at stage 1 to compare.
- `docs/version_1_status.md`: version 1's status page, frozen at the tag: `tests/test_version_1_status.py` checks that it is the tag's, byte for byte.
- `docs/review/phase_c_famdiag_seed7.json`: the seed-7 run as a family diagram (`tools/famdiag_export.py`); regenerated at revision 36 (no uncommitted edits to it stood in the way on 2026-10-23), so `git diff 7aa9fc5` on it shows the new illness events and any change of trajectory.
